## Ramo 3

ID: `dbranch_11d36fca80364391cf3225ae67a605456ed5410a6823775a7cb67d04ad7d2280`. Pagine: [69].

- Internal compressor and temperature LEDs illuminate → Internal compressor air inlet filter completely clogged → Replace the compressor’s air inlet filter

}

## Ramo 4

ID: `dbranch_223a765b5cac96ac9a8ca5efb0880f4ecdabe9e668feb5c93d0cafe177fcbe29`. Pagine: [71].

- Arc goes out during cutting or intermittently will not fire → Poor work lead connection → Reposition the work lead
- Arc goes out during cutting or intermittently will not fire → Poor work lead connection → Clean the cutting surface
- Arc goes out during cutting or intermittently will not fire → Poor work lead connection → Repair loose work lead connections if necessary

## Ramo 6

ID: `dbranch_3ec380a33020724399791baa4dfb6c82706f010cca347de4066f531f2343e9ff`. Pagine: [77].

- 4 blinks → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the solenoid valve
- 4 blinks → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the fan
- 4 blinks → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the power board

        "section": null,
        "source_block_index": 2,
        "table_index": null
      },
      "quote": "8. Connect the air pressure test gauge kit (428643) inside the power supply as shown in Figure 22:",

    },
    {

        "extraction_method": "native_text",
        "kind": "pdf",
        "ocr_region_index": null,
        "page": 102,
        "printed_page": null,
        "quote": "a. From the fan side of the power supply, push-to-release the 90° fitting\n from the torch lead gas supply fitting.",
        "row_index": null,
        "section": null,
        "source_block_index": 3,
        "table_index": null
      },
      "quote": "a. From the fan side of the power supply, push-to-release the 90° fitting from the torch lead gas supply fitting.",

## Ramo 11

ID: `dbranch_81256b08e52f492f199bc1ad95a48a68025f4566e8e9bbf5c91fffa1456c4d24`. Pagine: [69].

- The internal compressor LED and the temperature LED illuminate → The air inlet filter on the internal compressor is completely clogged → Replace the compressor’s air inlet filter

}

## Ramo 12

ID: `dbranch_8a28f81856a7c1d3418486f8e1c6407e0f43c7de4d80ea3b1f945ba86025242c`. Pagine: [77].

- Error LED blinks 4 times → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the solenoid valve
- Error LED blinks 4 times → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the fan
- Error LED blinks 4 times → Faulty fan, solenoid valve, or power board → Replace the power board

## Ramo 13

ID: `dbranch_959d292149487ec59021c68d02583ed595ed853fc4abfc36fdcc0d838ad66641`. Pagine: [67].

- The temperature LED blinks while the machine is powered ON → System continuously draws too much input current for too long → Let the system cool

            "quote": "This fault occurs when the system continuously draws too much input current for too long. The fault protects the power switch and power cord from damage caused by overheating. This fault most often occurs when:  You are operating the system on a 120 VAC input circuit and the cutting current is set above 20 A.  You are operating the system on a 120 V / 15 A circuit.  You are operating the system on a 120 VAC input circuit and are frequently stretching the arc while cutting.  You are using an extension cord that is too long. The maximum length for an extension cord on a 120 VAC input circuit is 16 m (53 feet). The maximum length for an extension cord on a 240 VAC input circuit is 40.5 m (133 feet). | Let the system cool for 3 minutes before using it. Leave the system on to allow the fan to cool the power supply. To prevent the fault from occurring:  Operate the system on a 240 VAC input circuit whenever possible.  Turn down the cutting current.
